# Task 02 — The unchanged skill is measured on the sixteen cells

Status: done

## Outcome
`evals/results/research/base-<dir>-<model>` hold ten runs per cell — eight case directories (four cases on the skill and the agent path) × sonnet and opus — against the unchanged `research` skill, loaded with `specs` and the `researcher` agent, on the instrument, sources and `evals/run.sh` bytes task 01 piloted, on the pilot's `claude` version, and within the shared $230 budget, so a later repair can be compared against them.

## Scope
- In: guards before any paid run; fifteen prerequisite invocations and the Command's one, each with `--keep-temp`, its group ceiling and a budget check before it, and at most one re-run per cell under the rule below; the summarizer, `read-traces.mjs` and an integrity check over the sixteen directories; the decisive text `read-traces.mjs` prints for every run failing a primary grader; saving every run's whole final answer to `evals/results/research/_answers/` before deleting this task's kept run directories; deleting them.
- Out: any change to `evals/` other than `evals/results/research/base-*` and `evals/results/research/_answers/`; any skill or agent; task 01's pilot directories; conclusions.

## Coverage
- CP-02

## Ownership
- Create: `evals/results/research/base-*` and `evals/results/research/_answers/` (both gitignored, `.gitignore:122`)
- Read: task 01's Receipt (its `evals-research-digest:`, `sources-digest:`, `run-sh-sha256:`, four `ceiling-<model>-<path>=` and `worst-case:` lines, the sixteen pilot `sha256:` lines, the pilots' findings), `specs/code-review-eval-baseline/task-*.md` (their `Status:` lines) and `task-01-with-agent-flag.md`'s Receipt (`run-sh-sha256:`), `evals/results/code-review/**/result.json` (their `costUsd`, for the budget), `evals/research/read-traces.mjs` (its primary-grader list)

## Steps
1. **Before any paid run**: none of the sixteen `base-*` directories and no `base-*-lan1` exists; every `specs/code-review-eval-baseline/task-*.md` still has `Status: done`; the `sha256` of `evals/run.sh` appears as `run-sh-sha256:` in both `specs/code-review-eval-baseline/task-01-with-agent-flag.md` and task 01's Receipt; the `evals/research` digest and the sources digest (`skills/research`, `skills/specs`, `agents/researcher.md` under `packages/spec/src/claude`) appear in task 01's Receipt as computed by the Command below; each pilot `result.json` still has the `sha256` its `Artifact:` line records (pilot directories are never moved; the completion gate hashes every `Artifact:` path, `specs/fix-eval-baseline/plan.md:15`); `node --version` prints `v20.20.2`, `command -v node` prints `/opt/homebrew/opt/node@20/bin/node`, `command -v git` prints `/opt/homebrew/bin/git`; `claude --version` equals the `claudeVersion` of the pilot `result.json` files; `node evals/research/ceiling.mjs <model> <path>` prints, for each of the four groups, the value task 01's `ceiling-<model>-<path>=` line shows; record `node evals/research/budget.mjs --worst-case`; export `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1`.
2. Run the fifteen prerequisite invocations — every cell except `base-chon-kien-truc-theo-rang-buoc-agent-opus` — two at a time, each `evals/run.sh research --with-skill specs --with-agent researcher --out base-<dir>-<model> --model <model> --judge-model sonnet --runs 10 --threshold 0 --ablation none --max-cost-usd <the ceiling of its model and path> --allow-tools Bash Edit Write WebSearch WebFetch --case <dir> --keep-temp`. Before each pair: `claude --version` still equals the pilot's `claudeVersion`, and `node evals/research/budget.mjs <ceiling of the first cell> <ceiling of the second cell>` exits 0 (the spending already recorded under `evals/results/code-review/` and `evals/results/research/` plus both ceilings stays within $230; a cell started alone passes its one ceiling) — otherwise stop and ask the user. After each, free: its `result.json` has `partial: false`, ten runs and no run with an `error` — otherwise apply Step 3 at once — and `node evals/research/read-traces.mjs <that directory>` exits 0; record its per-directory line. Then run the Command via `bash -c` on this file's own text. After the 2026-09-30 15:20 stop (user decision, plan Review log), the remaining cells and the Command run one at a time, each after `budget.mjs <its ceiling>`.
3. **Re-run rule** (D-05): a cell that exits non-zero with an error, has a run with an `error`, or comes back short because of an error is renamed to `base-<dir>-<model>-lan1` and run once more under the same name and ceiling, after the same budget check (for `base-chon-kien-truc-theo-rang-buoc-agent-opus`, by running the Command again). An `error` that is an account usage or session limit is not a failed cell: stop for the user at once, with no `-lan1` (plan D-05). The second result is kept whatever it shows: its errored runs are listed apart with their `error` and left out of every count, and timeouts are reported as a separate over-reach figure with the `chars`, tools and web calls `read-traces.mjs` prints for them. A cell that stops `partial` without any error — its ceiling was hit — is reported to the user and not re-run. A cell whose `read-traces.mjs` line shows a run with an `init` lacking a web tool uses the same one re-run (user decision, plan Review log); if it shows again, stop for the user. No cell runs a third time.
4. From the Command's `read-traces.mjs` output, record for every run failing a primary grader its run number and `decisive` lines, and for every agent-path cell the `report-src` and report-versus-final counts. Before deleting anything, save for GATE-DONE's hand reading (the plan's Known limits) the whole final answer of every run of every `base-*` cell (and any `-lan1` cell), rebuilt as `read-traces.mjs` rebuilds it (the texts of the last `assistant` event that carries text, joined by newlines), into `evals/results/research/_answers/<cell>.txt`: for every run of the cell, in order, a header line `### run <n>` — `<n>` being the 1-based index in `arms.with`, the `run=` number `read-traces.mjs` prints — followed by its answer verbatim; an errored run gets the header `### run <n> errored` followed by its answer when it has a trace, or by the line `(no trace)` when it has none; check that each file holds exactly as many lines matching `^### run [0-9]+( errored)?$` as its cell has runs. Then delete the kept directory of every run of this task, including any `-lan1` cell: `dirname(dirname(tracePath))` from its `result.json`, `chmod -R u+rwX` then `rm -rf` on that one path — never a glob, since other packets' kept directories may sit beside them in `/private/tmp`. Task 01's kept pilot directories stay until GATE-DONE.
5. Report each cell from the Command's output: every grader's count over the runs without an error; `co-goi-skill` or `co-goi-agent`; `noi-do-sau`, `co-nhan-claim`, every case grader and every `khong-*`; `dem-goi-research-khac`; per agent-path cell the `report-src` counts and the report-versus-final counts; `web-calls`, `web-ok` and `runs-with-web-ok`; `researcher-ok-runs`; `sub=` totals and any `last-from=sub` run; median `chars`; `timeouts=` with `timeout-chars=` as the separate over-reach figure; median tool calls; median seconds; `cost-with-judge` (the harness's `costUsd`, which includes the judge), `judge-cost` and `cost-without-judge`; `errored=`, `retried=`, `partial-no-error=` and `claudeVersion`; the total spent from `budget.mjs`. **No conclusion is drawn here.**
6. Write the Receipt with the Command's output, one `Artifact:` line per `base-*/result.json` (and any `-lan1`) with a `sha256:` line beneath it, one `Artifact:` line per `_answers/<cell>.txt` with a `sha256:` line directly beneath it and its header count on its own line after that (`headers: <n>`; anything between an `Artifact:` line and its `sha256:` line breaks the gate's hash check), and each prerequisite cell's exit, path, `sha256` and budget line, a failed first attempt's exit written in prose (`first attempt exited 1`), never as an `Exit:` field, since every `Exit:` line must read 0; set `Status: done`; then run the Stop gate for this packet — `printf '{"hook_event_name":"Stop","cwd":"%s","session_id":"manual-check","featureName":"research-eval-baseline"}' "$PWD" | CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="$PWD" node .claude/hooks/spec-gate.cjs` (`.claude/scripts/spec-resolver.cjs:550`) — and require **empty stdout** (`.claude/hooks/spec-gate.cjs:57-58`).

## What the history suggests
Written 2026-09-26, before any paid run, from the history review — not targets. `noi-do-sau` and `co-nhan-claim` low (depth missing in 6 of 7 v3 final answers, labels lost in 7 of 7); on the agent path, labels present in more researcher reports than final answers; case 1 over-reach in some runs (`khong-agent`, `do-dai-gon`, timeouts; 5 of 16 history episodes over-reached); case 3's `khong-*` mostly passing, while the one Claude Code episode wrote a report, edited plans and pushed; `trich-*` in `path:line` form, the web statement, the agent path's `report-src` and cases 2 and 4 unknown.

## Acceptance
- AC-02 as stated in `plan.md`.
- Every run failing a primary grader is named with its run number and decisive text.
- Every re-run cell, every errored or timed-out run and every cell stopped by its ceiling is named; errored runs are in no count.
- **No conclusion is drawn here.**

- Oracle: every guard passes; `budget.mjs` prints a `total` within 230; the invocation, the summarizer and `read-traces.mjs` exit 0; the integrity check prints sixteen lines with `instrument=current`, `partial-no-error=false` and either `partial=false runs=10 errored=0` or `retried=true`, and `claude-versions=1`
- Counterexample: an unfinished code-review packet, an `evals/run.sh` whose `sha256` is not in both Receipts, a changed file under `evals/research`, a changed byte under `skills/research`, `skills/specs` or `agents/researcher.md`, a missing, moved or changed pilot `result.json` (an empty hash no longer matches any `sha256:` line), another `node`, `git` or `claude`, a ceiling other than task 01's, or spending that would pass $230 each fail a guard before the paid run; a stored grader differing from `evals/research`, two `claude` versions, a cell stopped by its ceiling, or a partial, short or errored cell that was not re-run make the integrity check exit 1; a stored verdict a trace contradicts, or a run whose `init` lacks the agent, a web tool or the skill, makes `read-traces.mjs` exit 1
- Artifacts: the sixteen `base-*/result.json` and any `-lan1` one, and each `_answers/<cell>.txt`, gitignored, each on its own `Artifact:` line with a `sha256:` line directly beneath it (an answers file's `headers: <n>` line follows its `sha256:` line); the kept run directories are deleted in Step 4, one exact path at a time, after the answers are saved and counted

## Failure Protocol
On a failed Step or Verification Plan run: stop; do not widen scope, change the Command, or weaken a test; record observed versus expected; repair only the cited cause; after three failed rounds, stop and ask the user. A cell that fails under Step 3's re-run rule follows that rule, not a repair round. A failed `ceiling.mjs` or `budget.mjs`, a cell stopped by its ceiling, a guard showing that `evals/research`, the sources, `evals/run.sh` or the `claude` version changed since task 01, a code-review packet that is not done or has moved, or a kept run directory that vanished before the Command read it stops the task for the user — a paid re-run does not repair any of them. When the Command fails after its paid opus invocation for another reason and no `base-chon-kien-truc-theo-rang-buoc-agent-opus-lan1` exists, rename the cell to that name, which uses its one re-run, and run the Command again; if it fails again, or a `-lan1` already exists, stop and ask the user. Never move or delete a pilot directory or any file an `Artifact:` line names.

First attempt and its disposition: the driver first ran the cells two at a time. At 15:20 `read-traces.mjs` exited 1 on `base-khong-luu-khong-sua-agent-sonnet` (runs 2 and 9: the first `init` lists `WebSearch` but not `WebFetch`, 12 tools instead of 13; a later `init` in each run lists both) and, run by the controller, on `base-da-co-quyet-dinh-opus` (run 3: one `init`, without `WebFetch`; the run loaded only `WebSearch` through `ToolSearch`), and the driver stopped. Per the user's decision (plan Review log) both cells were renamed to `-lan1` and run once more under the same name and ceiling, and the remaining cells ran one at a time. None of the 100 runs started after that — 90 prerequisite runs and the Command's ten — has an `init` without a web tool. That running two cells at once caused the missing tool is a suspicion, not a verified cause. Nothing else was re-run: no run has an `error` (`errored=0` and no timeouts in every cell), and no cell stopped at its ceiling.

Step 4: the run number and `decisive` lines of every run failing a primary grader, and every agent-path cell's `report-src` and report-versus-final counts, are the per-run and per-directory `read-traces.mjs` lines in the block. Before anything was deleted, the whole final answer of every run of the sixteen cells and the two `-lan1` cells — rebuilt as `read-traces.mjs` rebuilds it, the texts of the last `assistant` event carrying text, joined by newlines — was saved to `evals/results/research/_answers/<cell>.txt` under `### run <n>` headers (no run errored, so no `errored` header); each file holds ten headers for its ten runs (the `headers:` lines below). Then the kept run directories of this task's 180 runs — every `dirname(dirname(tracePath))` of the eighteen cells, 180 distinct `/private/tmp/e-*` paths, none of them one of task 01's sixteen pilot directories — were deleted one exact path at a time with `chmod -R u+rwX` then `rm -rf`: all 180 were deleted and none remains (the loop printed `deleted=180 problems=0`, after checking each path's `/private/tmp/e-*` shape and that it was a directory). Task 01's pilot directories stay until GATE-DONE.

Step 5 report, per cell, from the block (grader passes over the ten runs, none errored, except `dem-goi-research-khac`, a `min: 0` counter that always passes, reported as its total calls and the runs with a call, read from each run's grader explanation; the other `dem-*` tool counters are in the block's summarizer lines; `report-vs-final` is in-report/in-final, agent path only). No conclusion is drawn here.
